```
│   │   ├── inventoryProficiencies.js # gatherProficiencies({charClass, characterData}) → {weapons/armor/tools: {text (CLASS_PROFICIENCIES_5E), grants (race_weapon/armor_proficiencies, race_tool_proficiency/proficiencies, background_tool_choice, tool_choice, subclass_tool_proficiencies)}} for the Items-tab proficiency banners. **Redundant-grant filtering**: a specific grant is dropped from `grants` when already covered by a broader proficiency the character has — from the class `text` OR another (broader) grant. A Fighter with "Simple weapons, martial weapons" drops racial Longsword/Shortbow grants (via SIMPLE/MARTIAL_WEAPONS category maps + weaponCategoryOf); a Bard drops a Drow Rapier grant its plural class list ("rapiers") already names; a Fighter with "All armor" drops a Dwarf Light/Medium armor grant; a class listing a tool drops that tool grant. A broad grant (Hexblade "Martial weapons") is kept unless the class TEXT already covers it (never self-covers), and a specific grant covered only by that broad grant is dropped. Dropping is safe for the equip/attack math — isWeaponProficient/isArmorProficient still detect proficiency via the broad category that covered the grant ✅
│   │   ├── levelChoicesData.js  # Generic "pick N from a pool at level L" class choices — the data-driven generalization of the subclass-proficiency + Battle Master maneuver pattern. LEVEL_CHOICES[class][edition] = [{key, label, storeField, knownAtLevel(level)→cumulative, pool:[{name,description,minLevel?}]}]. Helpers: getLevelChoices(class,edition,oldLevel,newLevel) (choices with a resolved per-level delta `count`), availablePoolOptions(choice,cd,level?) (hides held; when `level` passed, also hides options gated above it via `minLevel`), applyLevelChoice(choice,chosen,cd,replace?) → patch (merges chosen NAMES into character_data[storeField]; an optional `replace` name is removed first — **RAW replace-on-level-up**, e.g. a Warlock swapping an Eldritch Invocation). Two pools wired: **Sorcerer Metamagic** (METAMAGIC_OPTIONS + metamagicKnownAtLevel 2/3/4@3/10/17) and **Warlock Eldritch Invocations** (ELDRITCH_INVOCATIONS_5E/2024 + eldritchInvocationsKnownAtLevel — 5e none until L2, 2024 1 from L1; options carry `minLevel`). Both consolidate the per-class count/pool out of the hand-written sheets (Sorcerer/Warlock import from here so the in-sheet picker and the LevelUpWizard write the same array). Drives the LevelUpWizard `level-choices` step (with a `ReplaceOneSelect` swap control — pick one held option to replace, which frees a slot for one extra new pick); adding another class/choice is pure data entry. NOT yet modeled: full invocation prereqs (Pact Boon / cantrip / other-invocation deps — only `minLevel` is gated) — the sheet free-text input remains the fallback ✅
│   │   ├── levelChoicesData.test.js # metamagicKnownAtLevel thresholds, METAMAGIC_OPTIONS pool shape, getLevelChoices delta (2→3=2, 9→10/16→17=1, 3→4 none, 5.5e/2024 alias, non-choice class empty), availablePoolOptions hides held, applyLevelChoice merge+dedupe; eldritchInvocationsKnownAtLevel (5e none-until-L2 / 2024 from-L1), getLevelChoices Warlock invocation deltas, availablePoolOptions minLevel gating (hides L5 invocation at L2, shows at L15, no-filter when omitted); applyLevelChoice replace (swaps out a replaced option before adding) (20 tests)
│   │   ├── subclassGrants.js # Subclass features that, at a level, let the player CHOOSE from a pool — ONE model for both kinds QA'd: a **proficiency** (tool/skill/language — Battle Master "Student of War") AND a **class-pool pick** (Champion "Additional Fighting Style" — a 2nd Fighting Style, 5e L10 / 2024 L7). Consolidates the former subclassProficiencyData.js + subclassLevelChoices.js (merged while only two subclasses were wired). SUBCLASS_GRANTS[class][edition][subclass] = [{level,key,label,count,storeField,options:[{value,description?}],heldFrom(cd,ctx)→names,surface:'sheet'|'banner'}] — the per-grant `heldFrom` resolver absorbs the type-specific "already held" logic (heldTools via gatherProficiencies / heldSkills / heldLanguages / heldFightingStyles), `surface:'banner'` = the chosen value shows in the Items-tab proficiency banner (Student of War) not the subclass sheet block. Helpers: getSubclassGrants(…,level) (exact level — wizard step), getEarnedSubclassGrants(…,level) (≤ level — sheet display + owed-slot), availableGrantOptions(grant,cd,ctx) (options minus heldFrom), applyGrant(grant,chosen,cd) → patch merging into cd[storeField]. Drives the LevelUpWizard `subclass-grants` step + the ClassSheet display; adding another subclass (either kind) is pure data entry. NOT merged: levelChoicesData.js (class-scoped, cumulative, replace, minLevel — different shape) ✅
│   │   ├── subclassCasterData.js # Subclass-granted spellcasting (casters by SUBCLASS, not class): SUBCLASS_CASTERS[class][edition][subclass] + getSubclassCaster(class, edition, subclass, level?) (level-gates on the entry's unlockLevel when level passed). **Eldritch Knight (both editions)**: `{kind:'known', spellcastingAbility:'intelligence', unlockLevel:3, slotsForLevel (THIRD_CASTER_SLOTS — third-caster table, 9-wide, slots L3→4th-level @L19), cantripsKnownAt (2→3@10), spellsKnownAt (3@3…13@20), spellList:'Wizard', leveledSwapPerLevel:1, note}`. 2024 EK words its list as "prepared" but is mechanically the same fixed list swapped on level-up, so both editions are modeled as KNOWN casters (like the app's 2024 Sorcerer/Bard). **The editions differ in two ways, both modeled here:** (1) **School restriction — 5e only.** The 5e entry carries `restrictedSchools: ['Abjuration','Evocation']` + `freeSchoolLevels: [3,8,14,20]` + `anySlotsAt(level)` (1 at L3 → 4 at L20) + `restrictedSlotsAt(level)` (= spellsKnownAt − anySlots; at L3 that's 2 restricted + 1 any of the 3 known). The 2024 entry has `restrictedSchools: null` (the restriction was dropped). The category attaches to the **slot, not the spell's school** — a Shield (abjuration) chosen for the any-school slot still occupies it and may later swap for any school — so the slot is RECORDED at pick time in `character_data.ek_spell_slots` rather than inferred. Helpers: `ekSpellSlots(characterData)` (the sidecar map; missing = restricted) + `ekSpellsInSlot(knownSpells, slotMap, slot)` + `EK_SLOT_RESTRICTED`/`EK_SLOT_ANY`. (2) **Swap on level-up.** Both editions may replace one leveled spell whenever they gain a Fighter level (`leveledSwapPerLevel: 1`) — the New Spells step therefore shows on EVERY EK level-up, not just count-increase levels — and a 5e swap must stay in its slot's category. `cantripSwapPerLevel` is 1 in 2024, **0 in 5e** (5e cantrips are permanent once chosen). Consumers: ClassSheet (activeCaster), CasterSpellBlock kind:'known' (two-category lists for a restricted caster), LevelUpWizard New Spells targets + school-filtered pickers, CharacterDetail hasSpells, backend rest. Arcane Trickster = the next data entry. Tests: subclassCasterData.test.js (18) ✅
│   │   ├── subclassGrants.test.js # data integrity (Student of War tool/banner both editions, Additional Fighting Style sheet L10 5e / L7 2024, every grant has options:[{value}]+heldFrom), getSubclassGrants exact-level (2024 alias, none for unknown/no-subclass), getEarnedSubclassGrants ≤ level, availableGrantOptions (proficiency hides held tools via gatherProficiencies, pool excludes base fighting_style + already-picked, full pool when empty), applyGrant (merge + dedupe) (12 tests)
│   │   ├── InventoryTab.jsx     # CharacterDetail Items-tab body: combat summary (computed AC + attacks from equipped weapons — both fold in the character's fighting styles via gatherFightingStyles; an equipped weapon whose attack got a style bonus shows an emerald `attack-style-{uid}` note), category sub-tabs (Weapons/Armor/Gear/**Tools**/Potions/Magic Items/Food & Drink — Tools is a synthetic tab after Gear: shows entries where isToolEntry, Gear excludes them + ammo; its Add reuses the adventuring-gear picker), per-category owned-item rows (config subtitle/badges) with quantity stepper / equip (BODY ARMOR only) / attune / remove + Add (ItemPickerDialog); **Hands panel** (`hands-panel`, top of the Weapons tab): a **Main Hand** + **Off Hand** select (`hand-select-{slot}`) choosing the held weapon/shield (or Free); selecting calls assignHand → persists `hand`+`equipped`; a two-handed weapon fills both (off-hand slot locks to `hand-locked-off`); a free hand shows the unarmed-strike + "free to cast (somatic)" note (`hand-free-{slot}`); a **Versatile** weapon held one-handed shows a grip toggle button (`hand-grip-{slot}`, "Grip with two hands") that moves it to a two-handed grip (`hand:'both'` → larger die, off-hand locks; the main slot then shows "Use one hand" to revert); readOnly shows held items without selects. **Weapons + shields are held via the Hands panel, not per-row Equip buttons** — weapon rows carry no equip button; a shield row (Armor tab) shows a "Hold in Weapons → Hands" hint, no equip button; held weapons/shields show a `hand-badge-{uid}` (Main/Off/Both hands); **weapons are individual items — no quantity stepper** (the incoming inventory is run through `normalizeWeapons` so a "Handaxe ×2" stack shows as two separate, independently-holdable rows; the split persists on the next inventory edit); a Versatile weapon held one-handed with the other hand free uses its two-handed die in the attack summary; **proficiency banners** on Weapons/Armor/Tools tabs (gatherProficiencies → class text + race/chosen-tool grant badges); feat-granted weapon proficiencies (e.g. Tavern Brawler → "Improvised weapons") fold into the per-weapon proficiency check too, so an equipped granted weapon (Improvised Weapon) reads as proficient; inventory changes push onChange(patch) (autoSaveClassPatch); equip patches armor_class too; readOnly hides controls; not-proficient amber flag on equipped weapons/armor; **armor non-proficiency banner** (`armor-nonprof-warning`, above the combat summary via `nonProficientEquippedArmor`/`armorNonProficiencyNote` — the full STR/DEX-disadvantage + can't-cast consequence; the armor proficiency check folds in feat armor grants (`proficiencies.armor.grants`), so Heavily/Moderately Armored suppress it; every attack row also carries `(disadvantage)` + the reason via getAttacks); **weapon property badges** on every weapon row (`WeaponPropertyBadges` over `weaponBadges(e)` — category/handedness/properties, click any badge for its explanation, same as the encyclopedia + creation); **Heavy-weapon disadvantage warning** on heavy weapon rows **regardless of equipped state** (amber row note `inv-warning-{uid}`) plus the attack-summary `(disadvantage)` + `attack-warning-{uid}` for equipped ones, via `weaponAttackWarning` over the character's size (`creatureSize`) + `edition` — 5e Small creature, 2024 STR/DEX 13. **Armor Strength-requirement warning** on armor rows regardless of equipped state (amber `inv-str-warning-{uid}` via `armorStrengthNote` — "Requires Strength 13 (you have 11) — wearing it will reduce your speed by 10 ft" / "speed reduced by 10 ft while worn" once equipped). **Loading-property note** on loading weapon rows regardless of equipped state (`inv-loading-{uid}`) via `weaponLoadingNote(e,{feats,proficient,edition})` — amber one-attack cap, or emerald "Loading ignored (Crossbow Expert)" for a proficient crossbow with the feat; a "How the Loading property works" link (`loading-learn-more-{uid}`) renders inline inside each loading weapon's row note → the Loading mechanics page. **Spacing note** on every ranged weapon row (`weaponNeedsAmmo` or Thrown) via `spacing-note-{uid}`: "Ranged attacks have disadvantage while an enemy is within 5 ft" (emerald "No disadvantage … (Crossbow Expert)" when the character has that feat) + a `spacing-learn-more-{uid}` link → the Spacing mechanics page. **Savage Attacks note** (`savage-attacks-{uid}`, emerald) on every **melee** weapon row (`weapon_type === 'melee'`) when the character has the Half-Orc Savage Attacks trait (via raceCombatNotes). **Champion crit range** (`crit-range-{uid}`, gold) on every weapon row for a Champion Fighter (via `critRange`/`critRangeLabel` — "Crit 19–20 (Improved Critical)" at L3, "Crit 18–20 (Superior Critical)" at L15). **Eldritch Strike note** (`eldritch-strike-{uid}`, violet, via subclassCombatNotes) on every weapon row for an Eldritch Knight L10+ — the on-hit save-disadvantage rider. **Great Weapon Master bonus attack** (`gwm-bonus-{uid}`, emerald, via `greatWeaponMasterNote`) on every **melee** weapon row when the character has the GWM feat — the crit/kill → bonus melee attack reminder (both editions). **Ammunition lives under the Weapons tab** (ammunitionData.js): a dedicated "Ammunition" subsection (`ammunition-section`) lists each ammo stack (count + 0-clamped ±stepper `ammo-qty-{uid}` + remove) with an **Add Ammunition** button (`add-ammo-btn`, gear picker filtered to `isAmmoItem`, owned qty defaults to the bundle size, e.g. 20); each ranged weapon with the Ammunition property (`weaponNeedsAmmo`) renders an inline control (`weapon-ammo-{uid}`) — a chooser (`ammo-select-{uid}`, only when >1 matching stack; stores `ammo_uid` on the weapon), an "{name}: N remaining" readout (`ammo-count-{uid}`), and a **Use Ammunition** button (`use-ammo-{uid}`, −1, disabled at 0) — or a "No matching ammunition" note. Takes `characterData` + `edition` ✅
│   │   ├── InventoryTab.test.jsx # category tabs (incl. Tools), weapon/armor proficiency banners, Tools tab (tool proficiencies + owned tools, Gear excludes tools), computed AC from equipped armor, attacks for equipped weapon (to-hit/damage), empty state, picker add → onChange, equip patches inventory+armor_class, remove, quantity stepper (gear), weapons have no quantity stepper, stacked weapon splits into individual rows (each offered as a hand option, no per-row equip button), **Hands** (main+off slots render, free-hand unarmed/somatic note, selecting a weapon persists hand+equipped, a two-handed weapon fills both + locks the off slot, a held shield gives +2 AC + shows in the Hands panel, held weapon shows a hand badge, readOnly shows held items without selects, a one-handed Versatile weapon's grip button → `hand:'both'`, a gripped Versatile weapon shows the larger die + a "Use one hand" revert, no grip button for a non-versatile weapon), not-proficient flag (Wizard+Longsword), feat-granted weapon proficiencies in the banner (Weapon Master), Tavern Brawler improvised-weapon proficiency (equipped Improvised Weapon not flagged + "Improvised weapons" in the banner), heavy-weapon warnings (5e Small creature equipped Heavy → disadvantage on row + attack, Medium none, 2024 STR<13 → disadvantage, unequipped Heavy still warns on its row), weapon property badges render on a weapon row (Heavy/Two-handed), readOnly hides controls, attune cap disables 4th; **ammunition** (shown under Weapons not Gear, ammo count + Use button under a ranged weapon, none for a melee weapon, Use decrements the matched stack, disabled at 0 + "no matching ammunition" note, chooser when multiple stacks + selecting persists ammo_uid, Add Ammunition filters the picker to ammo + defaults to bundle qty, readOnly hides ammo controls); Loading note (`inv-loading-{uid}` cap note without the feat, "ignored" with Crossbow Expert, none in 2024) + per-row `loading-learn-more-{uid}` link (inside a loading weapon's row, absent on a non-loading weapon); Weapons-tab `object-interaction-learn-more` link to the drawing/stowing page; spacing note (`spacing-note-{uid}` + `spacing-learn-more-{uid}` on a ranged weapon — disadvantage text, "no disadvantage" with Crossbow Expert, also shown on a thrown weapon, absent on a melee-only weapon); Savage Attacks note (`savage-attacks-{uid}` on a melee weapon for a Half-Orc, absent on a ranged weapon, absent without the trait); Champion crit range (`crit-range-{uid}` "Crit 19–20 (Improved Critical)" on weapon rows for a L3 Champion, "Crit 18–20 (Superior Critical)" at L15, absent for a non-Champion / <L3); Great Weapon Master bonus attack (`gwm-bonus-{uid}` crit/kill bonus-attack note on a melee weapon with the feat, absent on a ranged weapon / without the feat); 2024 Champion Remarkable Athlete post-crit move (`remarkable-move-{uid}` "half your Speed" note on weapon rows for a L3+ 2024 Champion, absent in 5e / for a non-Champion); free-hand note reflects the feat unarmed die (Tavern Brawler → 1d4 + STR, else 1 + STR); armor Str-requirement warning (`inv-str-warning-{uid}` on an equipped unmet-Str armor row + the "will reduce" wording on an unequipped one, absent when met/no requirement); armor non-proficiency (banner for a Wizard in Chain Mail incl. can't-cast text, absent for a Fighter / with the Heavily Armored feat, weapon attacks show `(disadvantage)` + the reason); Eldritch Strike (`eldritch-strike-{uid}` on weapon rows for an EK L10, absent below L10 / non-EK); Weapon Bond (Bonded Weapons panel for an EK L3+, bonding persists bonded_weapon_uids + `bond-badge-{uid}`, hidden for Champion / below L3 / readOnly chooser); Hex Warrior (panel for a 5e Hexblade, designating persists hex_weapon_uid, Two-Handed excluded, hidden for other patrons, hex weapon attacks with CHA + `attack-hex-{uid}` note + `hex-badge-{uid}`) (79 tests)
│   │   ├── actionEconomyData.js # Action-economy model (pure): buckets what a SPECIFIC character can do into **no_action/action/bonus/action+bonus/reaction** (5 tabs; `no_action` = things OUTSIDE the action economy that cost no action/bonus/reaction). TABS lists no_action FIRST. UNIVERSAL_ACTIONS_5E/2024 + UNIVERSAL_REACTIONS_5E/2024 (the standard menu, edition-aware; "Cast a Spell"/"Magic" omitted here — spells surface as a single collapsed **"Cast a Spell"** entry per casting-time bucket (see below), not per-spell); CLASS_FEATURE_ACTIONS_5E/2024 (curated per-class feature→{tab,cost,description,resourceKey?}; **vertical slice = Fighter only**: Second Wind→bonus, Action Surge→**no_action**, Indomitable→**no_action** (both cost "no action"); descriptions are level-only — no "twice at L17"/"more at higher levels" overview text (the Use button shows the real count instead); `resourceKey` links rest-rechargeable features to the config's `restResources` so the Action Economy tab can render a Use button; other classes get only auto-derived entries until authored); RACIAL_ACTIONS (Breath Weapon→action); **SUBCLASS_FEATURE_ACTIONS_5E/2024** (curated per-SUBCLASS feature→entry map, level-gated from the SUBCLASS_DATA feature tables via exported `subclassFeaturesKnownAtLevel(class,edition,subclass,level)` — the class tables only carry placeholders at subclass levels so they can never match; entries push with source 'Subclass'; first wired: **Eldritch Knight Weapon Bond** → bonus action (RAW up to 2 bonded weapons both editions). **When weapons are actually bonded** (`character_data.bonded_weapon_uids`, via bondedWeapons over the inventory), buildActionEconomy replaces the generic entry with one **"Bonded {Name}"** entry per bonded weapon (source 'Subclass', carrying the weapon's damage info + a live attack `subAttacks` row when that weapon is equipped); with nothing bonded, the generic entry stays with a hint pointing at the Items-tab picker. **Hex Warrior** (Hexblade): a weapon Action entry carries a `hexNote` when its attack row used Charisma (from getAttacks). **Eldritch Knight combos/riders** (curated blocks in buildActionEconomy): **War Magic** (L7) — an `action+bonus` combo (`key:'war-magic'`, source 'Subclass', gated on a real equipped-weapon attack row): subAttacks = "Cast a cantrip" Action row + the equipped weapon's real Bonus attack; at L18 the same entry upgrades level-keyed to "Cast a spell" (Improved War Magic) — the detail notes casting replaces the Attack action (no Extra Attack that turn); **Arcane Charge** (L15) — appended to the existing Action Surge entry's detail (a rider, not its own action): teleport up to 30 ft before/after the extra action. **Feat effects** (via featEffects.js): feat `action` effects are added to their bucket with source "Feat" (e.g. Tavern Brawler's bonus-action grapple), and a feat `attack_mod` unarmed die (e.g. Tavern Brawler 1d4) is shown on the Unarmed Strike row even when a weapon is equipped. Helpers: classifyCastingTime (casting_time str → tab+cost, bonus-before-action; null for >1-turn), characterSpellNames (cantrips+prepared+known union, dedupe, excludes spellbook), normalizeFeatureName (strips trailing "(1/rest)"/"(2/LR)" so feature-table names match the map; keeps "Indomitable Might" distinct), featuresKnownAtLevel (distinct normalized features ≤ level), attacksPerAction (Fighter 5/11/20 tiers; default 1), twoWeaponFightingWeapons(inventory, feats) (the equipped weapons qualifying for TWF — light melee, or with **Dual Wielder** any one-handed/non-two-handed melee; returns [main, off] ordered by the weapons' `hand` when both hands are assigned, so the off-hand bonus attack is explicit) + canTwoWeaponFight(inventory, feats) (≥2 such weapons); the TWF entry carries a `subAttacks` array — explicit **Main hand / Off hand** rows ({label,name,toHit,damage,warning}) drawn from the precomputed `attacks` rows; the off-hand row drops the ability modifier from its damage unless `character_data.fighting_style === 'Two-Weapon Fighting'`, and the detail notes when Dual Wielder lifts the light requirement; **Crossbow Expert** (feat) is likewise surfaced as an `action+bonus` combo (`key:'crossbow-expert'`, source "Feat") when a hand crossbow is equipped — subAttacks are an **Action** row (the equipped one-handed melee weapon, else the hand crossbow itself) + a **Bonus** row (the hand crossbow, keeping its ability mod, unlike TWF); the feat's bonus-action `action` effect is never shown standalone — it appears ONLY as this combo (so with no hand crossbow equipped, Crossbow Expert shows in no bucket at all). isHandCrossbow matches "Hand Crossbow"/"Crossbow, Hand". **Tavern Brawler** is likewise an `action+bonus` combo (`key:'tavern-brawler'`, source "Feat") whenever the character carries the feat's grapple `action` effect: subAttacks are an **Action** row (an equipped **Improvised Weapon** via `isImprovisedWeapon` — weapon_category "Improvised" or name match — else the feat-upgraded **Unarmed Strike**) + a **Bonus** row (**Grapple**, the feat action's description as `detail`); the standalone bonus grapple is suppressed (shown only as this combo). The Tavern Brawler combo is **equipment-gated** — only shown when its Action half is actually makeable: an equipped improvised weapon, or a free hand (`freeHandCount > 0`) for the Unarmed Strike; hidden when both hands are full with no improvised weapon. **Charger** (feat, **5e only** — the 2024 feat is a different mechanic, a damage rider on the Attack action) is likewise an `action+bonus` combo (`key:'charger'`, source "Feat", gated on `hasFeat(feats,'Charger')`): the Action row is **Dash**; with a melee weapon equipped the subAttacks are Dash + a **Bonus** attack (the equipped melee weapon's real to-hit/damage) + an **or Shove** row (the +5 damage / 10-ft push rider lives in the entry `detail`); with no melee weapon equipped only Dash + a **Bonus** Shove show. **In 2024** Charger is a different mechanic, so instead it adds a distinct **Charge** entry to the **Action** bucket (`key:'charger-2024'`, source "Feat"), gated on 2024 + `hasFeat` + a melee weapon equipped: subAttacks are the equipped weapon **Attack** (real to-hit/damage) + a **+1d8** row + an **or Push 10 ft** row, with the once-per-turn / move-10-ft / Dash-+10-ft context in the entry `detail`. Other equipment-dependent feat `action` effects are likewise gated in the generic feat-action loop: **Polearm Master**'s bonus attack is hidden unless a `isPolearm` weapon (glaive/halberd/quarterstaff/spear) is equipped, **Defensive Duelist**'s reaction is hidden unless a `isFinesseWeapon` is equipped (when shown its detail spells out the "+PB AC" it adds), and **Great Weapon Master**'s crit/kill bonus attack is shown BOTH as a standalone Bonus entry (gated on a melee weapon equipped, both editions) AND as a `greatWeaponMasterNote` reminder ON each melee weapon Action entry, co-located with the power attack. **Great Weapon Master power attack** (5e only — the 2024 feat has no −5/+10 option): a proficient Heavy melee weapon's Action entry carries a `powerAttack` variant {toHit,toHitBreakdown,detailRest} via `greatWeaponMasterVariant(attackRow)` (a pure exported helper: −5 to-hit, +10 to the flat damage modifier parsed out of the row's damage string), which the UI toggles between normal and power numbers; `powerAttack` is null when the feat/weapon/proficiency conditions aren't met. buildActionEconomy({...,attacks,spellIndex,armorProfText,raceArmor}) → {no_action,action,bonus,'action+bonus',reaction,attacksPerAction} (entries {key,name,source,cost,detail}; weapons→Action via passed-in getAttacks rows + Unarmed Strike fallback — the fallback is itself a STR-based attack roll, so worn non-proficient armor (via nonProficientEquippedArmor over the armor args) flags it with disadvantage + the reason (equipped-weapon rows already carry this from getAttacks), the detail appends `· not proficient` / `· disadvantage` from the row's flags + carries the full `warning` string for the amber note + the row's `loadingNote` (Loading-property cap / "ignored (Crossbow Expert)") + a `powerAttack` variant when GWM applies + a `spacingNote` on ranged/thrown weapon entries (via `isRangedWeapon` — Ammunition/weapon_type ranged, or Thrown; "disadvantage while an enemy is within 5 ft", flipped to "No disadvantage … (Crossbow Expert)" when the feat is held; null for melee — the within-5-ft ranged-attack rule, linked to the Spacing page) + a `savageAttacksNote` on melee weapon entries when the character has the Half-Orc **Savage Attacks** trait (via raceCombatNotes; null for ranged/unarmed) + a `greatWeaponMasterNote` on melee weapon entries when the character has **Great Weapon Master** (the crit/kill bonus-attack reminder; null for ranged/unarmed) + `critRange`/`critSource` on real weapon Action entries when the character is a **Champion Fighter** (via `critRange`; Improved Critical 19–20 at L3, Superior Critical 18–20 at L15; null for non-Champions and the unarmed fallback) + an `eldritchStrikeNote` on real weapon Action entries for an **Eldritch Knight L10+** (via subclassCombatNotes; null for the unarmed fallback); TWF→Action+Bonus; **spells are collapsed** — rather than one entry per known spell, a single **"Cast a Spell"** entry (source 'Spell', `key:'spell:{tab}'`, detail "See the Spells tab…") is pushed into each of action/bonus/reaction that the character has ≥1 castable spell in (classified via spellIndex+casting_time), keeping the tab uncluttered while preserving the bonus/reaction-casting info; the group-level `ae-spacing-spells`/`ae-armor-spells` notes still attach via source 'Spell') ✅
│   │   ├── actionEconomyData.test.js # classifyCastingTime, characterSpellNames union/dedupe, attacksPerAction (Fighter tiers + default), canTwoWeaponFight, normalizeFeatureName + featuresKnownAtLevel (Fighter L9 deduped), buildActionEconomy (Fighter 5e/2024: weapons+universal Attack, Second Wind→bonus, Action Surge→no_action (not action), Indomitable→no_action@L9 with Opportunity Attack staying a reaction, Action Surge desc has no "twice at L17" overview text, rest features tagged with resourceKey, empty Action+Bonus, spells collapse to one "Cast a Spell" entry per casting-time bucket (+ none when the character can't cast), unarmed fallback, TWF, Dragonborn Breath Weapon; feat effects — Tavern Brawler grapple as an Action+Bonus combo (Unarmed Strike Action + Grapple Bonus, no standalone bonus grapple) + equipped Improvised Weapon as the Action half, feat unarmed 1d4 shown even with a weapon, no Feat entries/unarmed row without feats, a disadvantaged weapon row appends `· disadvantage` to its detail, Dual Wielder enables TWF with two non-light one-handed weapons (and not without the feat / not with a two-handed weapon), the TWF entry exposes Main hand / Off hand subAttacks (off-hand drops the ability mod unless the Two-Weapon Fighting fighting style is set), ranged weapons never enable TWF — with or without Dual Wielder; Crossbow Expert — the feat becomes an Action+Bonus combo (melee Action → hand-crossbow Bonus keeping its ability mod), shows in NO bucket when no hand crossbow is equipped (no standalone bonus entry) or without the feat, and uses the hand crossbow as the Action weapon when no melee is equipped; carries a weapon row's loadingNote onto its Action entry; weapon + unarmed entries carry the `toHitBreakdown` + `detailRest` so the UI can render a clickable to-hit; equipment-gated feat actions — Tavern Brawler combo hidden when both hands are full with no improvised weapon (shown when a hand is free), Polearm Master bonus attack hidden unless a glaive/halberd/quarterstaff/spear is equipped, Defensive Duelist reaction hidden unless a finesse weapon is equipped + shows the "+PB AC" it adds; Great Weapon Master — the crit/kill bonus attack shows as a standalone Bonus entry gated on a melee weapon equipped (hidden with none) AND a `greatWeaponMasterNote` rides each melee weapon Action entry both editions (null on a ranged weapon / unarmed fallback / without the feat), a proficient Heavy melee weapon carries a −5/+10 `powerAttack` variant (not without the feat / non-heavy / not-proficient / not in 2024), greatWeaponMasterVariant helper subtracts 5 to-hit + adds 10 to the flat damage mod incl. no-existing-mod and negative-mod cases; spacing note — a ranged weapon entry gets a within-5-ft disadvantage `spacingNote` (flipped to "no disadvantage" with Crossbow Expert), a thrown weapon gets one too, a melee-only weapon gets null); Savage Attacks note — a melee weapon entry carries `savageAttacksNote` for a Half-Orc (null on a ranged weapon / without the trait); Champion crit range — a real weapon Action entry carries `critRange`/`critSource` (Improved Critical 19–20 at L3, Superior Critical 18–20 at L15; null for non-Champions and the unarmed fallback); 2024 Champion Remarkable Athlete post-crit move — a real weapon Action entry carries `remarkableMoveNote` for a L3+ 2024 Champion (null in 5e / non-Champion / unarmed fallback); Charger (5e) — a Dash + attack/shove Action+Bonus combo when a melee weapon is equipped, only Dash + Shove without one, absent without the feat, absent in 2024; Charger (2024) — a "Charge" Action entry (weapon attack + 1d8/push options) when a melee weapon is equipped, absent without a weapon / without the feat / in 5e; armor non-proficiency — the Unarmed Strike fallback gets `· disadvantage` + the reason while wearing non-proficient armor, clean when proficient; Eldritch Knight — subclassFeaturesKnownAtLevel reads SUBCLASS_DATA levels, Weapon Bond as a Subclass bonus action from L3 (up to two weapons both editions, absent for Champion/below L3), War Magic Action+Bonus combo at L7 (cantrip Action + real weapon Bonus row, level-keyed upgrade to any spell at L18, hidden below L7/without a weapon/non-EK), Arcane Charge rider appended to Action Surge at L15 only, eldritchStrikeNote rides real weapon entries at L10 (null below L10/unarmed/non-EK); Weapon Bond bonded weapons — a bonded weapon replaces the generic entry with "Bonded {Name}" carrying its damage info + a live attack subAttack row when equipped, two bonds → two entries, generic entry with a picker hint when nothing bonded; Hex Warrior — a weapon attack entry carries the hexNote from its attack row) (89 tests)
│   │   ├── ActionEconomyTab.jsx # CharacterDetail "Action Economy" tab body: **5 button sub-tabs (No Action/Actions/Bonus/Action+Bonus/Reactions — No Action first** — NOT Radix Tabs; default active = Actions); computes weapon attacks via getAttacks (same inputs as InventoryTab, incl. `size` via `creatureSize` + `edition` so a heavy-weapon attack carries the disadvantage flag), fetches the spell catalog via encyclopediaService ONLY when the character knows spells (indexes name→{casting_time,level,school}), then buildActionEconomy. Groups character-specific entries by source (Weapon→"Weapon Attacks", Class Feature, Spell, Racial…) above a secondary "General — available to everyone" universal group; Extra Attack note on Actions when attacksPerAction>1; per-tab empty note when a bucket is empty. **Rest-rechargeable feature entries (those with a `resourceKey`) render an inline Use button** (`RestResourceControl` from RestResourceTracker, idPrefix `ae-rest`) tied to the config's `restResources` (via getClassConfig + useRestResource) — same Use/−/confirm-dialog mechanic as the Features tab; takes `onChange` (CharacterDetail wires `autoSaveClassPatch` → immediate persist of `<key>_used`) + `readOnly`. An equipped weapon that imposes disadvantage (Heavy + Small in 5e, or STR/DEX<13 in 2024, or **worn non-proficient armor** — the tab passes `armorProfText`/`raceArmor` to getAttacks) shows an amber warning under its entry (`ae-warning-{key}`) — same `weaponAttackWarning`/non-proficiency merge as the Items tab. The **Spell** source section shows an amber "you can't cast spells while wearing X" note (`ae-armor-spells`, via `nonProficientEquippedArmor`) when non-proficient armor is worn. A **Loading** weapon shows a per-entry note (`ae-loading-{key}` — amber cap / emerald "ignored (Crossbow Expert)") with an inline "How the Loading property works" link (`loading-learn-more-{key}`) → the Loading mechanics page, and, when the Extra Attack note is shown and a still-capped loading weapon is equipped, an amber caveat on that note (`ae-loading-caveat`: "A loading weapon (X) can still be fired only once per action") — since the app displays attacks rather than rolling them, this annotation is the enforcement. A Two-Weapon Fighting entry (and the Crossbow Expert / Tavern Brawler combos) renders its `subAttacks` as labeled weapon rows (name + to-hit · damage, `· disadvantage` when warned) — the subAttacks render is generic (any entry with subAttacks); a sub-row with no to-hit/damage renders its `detail` instead (the Tavern Brawler Grapple Bonus row), so the Crossbow Expert + Tavern Brawler rows show without extra UI code. A weapon (and unarmed) attack's **to-hit is a clickable chip** (`ToHitBreakdown`, `ae-tohit-{key}`) — tapping it toggles a breakdown of how the +N is calculated (ability mod, Proficiency, each fighting style, e.g. "+3 DEX, +4 Proficiency, +2 Archery fighting style"), shown in `ae-tohit-breakdown-{key}`; falls back to plain text when an entry has no `toHitBreakdown`. A weapon entry with a `powerAttack` variant (Great Weapon Master, 5e) shows a **toggle button** (`ae-gwm-toggle-{key}`) that swaps the displayed to-hit/damage between the normal and −5/+10 numbers (local per-row state). A **ranged/thrown weapon entry** shows a spacing note (`ae-spacing-{key}` — muted "disadvantage within 5 ft" / emerald "no disadvantage (Crossbow Expert)") with an inline "How spacing works" link (`spacing-learn-more-{key}`) → the Spacing mechanics page; the **Spell** source section shows a group-level note (`ae-spacing-spells` + `spacing-learn-more-spells`) that a ranged-spell-attack roll has disadvantage within 5 ft (per-spell attack-roll detection isn't derivable from the indexed spell data, so the note is section-level). A **melee weapon entry** shows an emerald **Savage Attacks note** (`ae-savage-{key}`, from the entry's `savageAttacksNote`) when the character has the Half-Orc trait. A **weapon attack entry** shows a violet **Eldritch Strike note** (`ae-eldritch-{key}`, from the entry's `eldritchStrikeNote`) for an Eldritch Knight L10+ — the on-hit save-disadvantage rider; the EK's **Weapon Bond** (Subclass bonus action), **War Magic** Action+Bonus combo, and **Arcane Charge** Action Surge rider all render through the generic entry/subAttacks machinery (no EK-specific UI code). A **weapon attack entry** shows a gold **crit-range note** (`ae-crit-{key}`, "Crit 19–20 (Improved Critical)" / "Crit 18–20 (Superior Critical)") when the character is a Champion Fighter (from the entry's `critRange`/`critSource`). A **melee weapon entry** shows an emerald **Great Weapon Master bonus-attack note** (`ae-gwm-{key}`, from the entry's `greatWeaponMasterNote`) — the crit/kill → bonus melee attack reminder, co-located with the power-attack toggle, in addition to the standalone "Cleave (Bonus Attack)" entry in the Bonus Actions bucket. data-testid ae-subtab-{tab} (incl. ae-subtab-no_action), ae-resource-{key}, ae-rest-use-confirm-button, ae-empty, ae-universal, ae-attacks-per-action, ae-warning-{key}, ae-tohit-{key}, ae-tohit-breakdown-{key}, ae-gwm-toggle-{key}, ae-gwm-{key}, ae-spacing-{key}, ae-spacing-spells, ae-savage-{key}, ae-crit-{key}, ae-hex-{key} (Hex Warrior CHA note), ae-twf-{label} (ae-twf-main-hand / ae-twf-off-hand for TWF; ae-twf-action / ae-twf-bonus for Crossbow Expert + Tavern Brawler) ✅
│   │   ├── ActionEconomyTab.test.jsx # 5 sub-tabs render (No Action first), Action Surge under No Action not Actions (L2), Use button for Second Wind (Bonus) + Action Surge (No Action) persists `<key>_used` via onChange, Use disabled when exhausted, readOnly hides Use, a player sees no `− recover` button for a spent feature (spend-only) while the GM (`isGm`) gets one that corrects the count, weapon attack + universal Attack on Actions, Extra Attack note (L5 Fighter), Second Wind under Bonus, Indomitable under No Action + Opportunity Attack under Reactions (L9), no spell fetch when none known, known spells collapse to one "Cast a Spell" entry per casting-time bucket (individual Fireball/Healing Word/Shield no longer listed), TWF on Action+Bonus renders Main hand / Off hand weapon rows (`ae-twf-main-hand`/`ae-twf-off-hand`), empty note on Action+Bonus otherwise, Heavy weapon for a Small 5e character flags `· disadvantage` on its detail, Loading weapon shows the cap note (`ae-loading-*`) + Extra Attack caveat (`ae-loading-caveat`) without the feat / "ignored" + no caveat with Crossbow Expert, per-entry `loading-learn-more-{key}` link to the Loading page, clickable to-hit breakdown (Archery longbow — chip shows the total, hidden until clicked, then lists +DEX/+Proficiency/+Archery, collapses on re-click), Tavern Brawler grapple combo on Action+Bonus (Unarmed Strike Action 1d4 + Grapple Bonus rendering its detail), Great Weapon Master power-attack toggle swaps a Heavy melee weapon to −5/+10 (and back) / no toggle without the feat, GWM crit/kill bonus-attack note (`ae-gwm-{key}`) on the melee weapon entry with the feat + also a standalone Cleave Bonus entry + absent without the feat; spacing notes — ranged weapon entry shows the within-5-ft disadvantage note + `spacing-learn-more-{key}` link (flipped to "no disadvantage" with Crossbow Expert), no note on a melee weapon, and a ranged-spell `ae-spacing-spells` note + link under the Spell section; Savage Attacks note (`ae-savage-{key}` on a melee weapon entry for a Half-Orc, absent without the trait); Champion crit range (`ae-crit-{key}` "Crit 19–20 (Improved Critical)" on a weapon entry for a L3 Champion, absent for a non-Champion); 2024 Champion Remarkable Athlete post-crit move (`ae-remarkable-move-{key}` "half your Speed" note on a weapon entry for a L3+ 2024 Champion, absent in 5e / for a non-Champion); armor non-proficiency (Wizard in Chain Mail → weapon entry `· disadvantage` + reason, unarmed fallback `· disadvantage` when no weapon equipped, `ae-armor-spells` can't-cast note under the Spell section, absent for a proficient Fighter); Eldritch Knight (Weapon Bond bonus entry at L3 + absent for Champion, War Magic combo renders cantrip Action + weapon Bonus rows on Action+Bonus, `ae-eldritch-{key}` note at L10 + absent below/non-EK, Arcane Charge rider on Action Surge at L15); Weapon Bond (a bonded weapon renders "Bonded {Name}" on Bonus Actions replacing the generic entry, generic entry with a picker hint when nothing bonded); Hex Warrior (`ae-hex-{key}` CHA note on the hex weapon entry for a Hexblade, absent for a non-Hexblade with the same stored uid) (51 tests)
│   │   ├── CombatBonusInline.jsx # Exports MaxHpValue (the Max HP cell's value — shows EFFECTIVE max HP = `baseMaxHp` (the CON-independent roll base) + `conMod × level` + passive bonuses, with a small "+N Source" note for the PASSIVE bonuses only (CON is part of the base number, not annotated); "—" when no base; never mutates stored HP) and AcOptionsLine (non-armor AC formulas; null when none — **no longer rendered anywhere**: AC moved out of the Stats tab to the Items tab, which surfaces the formula via its computed AC summary; the component + its tests are kept but unused). All sheets accept a `maxHpNode` render slot (default null): the Max HP cell renders `{maxHpNode ?? (data.hp_max ?? '—')}`; CharacterDetail builds it and passes it only to the stats-section sheet, so the bonus is folded into the value it modifies (not a separate card/row). (Sheets still accept a vestigial `acExtra` slot that is no longer rendered.) Filename differs from combatBonuses.js by more than case (Windows self-import safety) ✅

│   │   ├── OptionCardPicker.jsx # Reusable card-based picker: each option shows name + 1-line description; replaces plain <select> for fighting styles, subclasses, pact boons; accepts optional `onDetailClick` prop that adds an Info button per card; clicking the already-selected card deselects it (`onChange('')`) so the user can clear a choice and compare other options. **The Info button is an absolutely-positioned SIBLING of the card-select button, never nested inside it** — a `<button>` inside a `<button>` is invalid HTML that the browser repairs by closing the outer button mid-card, breaking the layout (cards spilled outside the dialog) ✅
│   │   ├── FeatPicker.jsx       # Reusable feat selection control: trigger button (`{prefix}-select`) opens a searchable Dialog listing every feat with full description + amber prerequisite (browse/compare before choosing); clicking a feat (`{prefix}-option-{id}`) selects it via onChange({id,name}); chosen feat's description stays visible below the trigger (`{prefix}-detail`); optional `getDisabledReason(feat)` prop — when it returns a reason the feat is shown non-selectable ("Locked" badge + `{prefix}-locked-{id}` reason note) and sorted to the bottom of the list (used by LevelUpWizard for unmet prerequisites); used by CharacterCreate Variant Human (testIdPrefix "human-feat"); replaces the old bare <select> ✅
│   │   ├── FeatSpellGrantPicker.jsx # Acquisition picker for a `spell_grant` feat (Magic Initiate): a source `<select>` (`{prefix}-source` — a class for source_kind 'class', or Arcane/Divine/Primal for 'group'), a spellcasting-ability `<select>` when the feat allows the choice (`{prefix}-ability`; derived + shown read-only for a class via `{prefix}-ability-derived`), then count-limited cantrip + per-level spell toggle grids filtered to the chosen list (`{prefix}-cantrip-{name}` / `{prefix}-leveled-{level}-{name}`); reports `{source, ability, cantrips:[names], leveled:[{name,level}], free_cast}` (the leveled spell is the 1/long-rest free cast). Always-granted `fixed:[{name,level}]` spells (Telekinetic/Telepathic/Fey/Shadow) render read-only (`{prefix}-fixed`); a leveled slot with a `school:[...]` filter (Fey/Shadow) is chosen from any spell of that school (no class list); a `ritual: true` slot (Ritual Caster) filters the class list to ritual spells and resolves to a growable `ritual_book`; pure-fixed/school feats need no source/ability pick so they auto-complete. Exports CLASS_SPELL_ABILITY / GROUP_CLASSES maps + `spellGrantComplete(spec, value)` (gates Next; a source list is required only for cantrips or non-school leveled slots) + `resolveSpellGrantValue(spec, value)` (final snapshot = picks + `fixed` + a `free_casts:[names]` list = every leveled granted spell when `free_cast` is set; used at acquisition so fixed/school feats store without interaction). Changing the source resets the picks ✅

│   │   ├── FeatSpellsSection.jsx # Spells-tab "Feats" content: getFeatGrantedSpells(feats) → cantrips+leveled in SpellLevelTabs + a **1/long-rest free-cast tracker** (RestResourceControl per freeCast, key `feat_freecast_<slug>_used`, owner Use persists via onChange; the `− recover` button is GM-only via the `isGm` prop, idPrefix `feat-freecast`) + a **growable Ritual Book** per Ritual Caster feat (editable `SpellList` over `choices.spell_grant.ritual_book`; add/remove persists onto the feat instance via an `{feats}` patch; cast as rituals only, no free cast); renders null when no feat grants spells/rituals. A **`showSpellTabs={false}`** prop renders ONLY the trackers (ritual books + free casts), skipping the per-level SpellLevelTabs — used when an outer level strip already lists the feat spells (the EK Spells-tab fold via `featTrackers`). data-testid feat-spells-section / feat-freecast-{name} / ritual-book-{source} (9 tests in FeatSpellsSection.test.jsx: empty, cantrips+leveled+tracker, showSpellTabs={false} trackers-only, Use persists slug key, GM recovers an already-used cast (`− is GM-only`), a player sees no `− recover` button, readOnly hides controls, ritual book add/remove persists onto the feat, readOnly hides ritual add/remove)
│   │   ├── featPrerequisites.js # Reusable feat-prerequisite parser/checker: parsePrerequisite(text) → ability/spell/armor/level requirement objects (fail-open on unrecognized text); checkFeatPrerequisite(feat, ctx) → {met, unmet:[{kind, dependsOn:'ability'|'class'|'level', reason}]}; null ctx fields skip that bucket (not knowable yet). Drives Variant Human feat gating in CharacterCreate + LevelUpWizard ASI-level feat eligibility ✅
│   │   ├── featEffects.js       # Feat effects resolver — turns a character's feats (with snapshotted `effects`) into mechanics so a feat is more than a description card. Helpers: allFeatEffects, getFeatStatMods(feats,stat,{pb}) (initiative/passive_perception/speed; an `amount` of `'pb'` resolves against the passed proficiency bonus — 2024 Alert), getFeatStatModSources, getFeatActions(feats) (Action Economy — Tavern Brawler grapple), getFeatUnarmedDice(feats) (Tavern Brawler 1d4), getFeatResources(feats,{pb}) (rest pools — Lucky/Martial Adept; a `total` of `'pb'` scales with the proficiency bonus — 2024 Lucky), getFeatProficiencyGrants(feats) (fixed armor/weapon/tool/language), featGrantRedundant(feat, {armorProficiencies, weapons:{simple,martial}}) (reason a half-feat's proficiency grant is already covered → the picker locks it as a trap pick: Lightly/Moderately/Heavily Armored when that armor tier is held, Weapon Master when the class has all simple+martial weapons, Martial Weapon Training (2024) when the class already has martial weapons; null if it still grants something new), getFeatSaveProficiencies(feats) (Resilient, via choices.ability), getFeatAcMods(feats) (conditional AC — Defense/Dual Wielder/Medium Armor Master; evaluated by computeArmorClass), featAbilityChoices(feat)/featFixedAbilityScores(feat) (half-feat ASI), featAbilityChoiceOptions(feat, choice, {saveProficiencies}) + abilityChoiceGrantsSave(feat) (Resilient's chooser only offers abilities whose saving throw the character lacks — a save-granting ability_choice; non-save half-feats keep the full list), getSpellGrantSpecs(feat) (the `spell_grant` spec the player fulfils at acquisition — Magic Initiate: {source_kind:'class'|'group', cantrips, leveled:[{level,count}], fixed, free_cast, ability}) + getFeatGrantedSpells(feats) (the picked spells read from each feat instance's `choices.spell_grant` snapshot → {cantrips, leveled, freeCasts}), getManeuverGrantSpec(feat)/maneuverGrantComplete(spec, chosen) (the `maneuver_grant` spec the player fulfils at acquisition — Martial Adept: {count, die}) + getFeatManeuvers(feats) (maneuvers read from each instance's `choices.maneuvers` → [{name,die,source}]) + martialAdeptDieCount(feats)/martialAdeptManeuverCount(feats) (the +dice / +maneuver-slots a Battle Master folds into Combat Superiority), isMechanized(feat) (drives the coverage report). Effects are authored on the backend feat (feats.effects) + snapshotted onto character_data.feats[i] at acquisition (inventory pattern) ✅
│   │   ├── featEffects.test.js  # flatten + source-tag, no-effects safety, stat_mod sum + sources, getFeatActions, getFeatUnarmedDice (largest die), getFeatResources (deduped), 'pb' stat_mod/resource scaling resolves with the proficiency bonus, getFeatProficiencyGrants (fixed only), getFeatSaveProficiencies (from_ability_choice), getFeatAcMods (conditional AC), featAbilityChoices, featFixedAbilityScores, isMechanized (+spell_grant counts), getSpellGrantSpecs (Magic Initiate spec), getFeatGrantedSpells (picked spells from choices.spell_grant incl. freeCasts usedKey + always-granted fixed spells + an editable ritualBook excluded from leveled; empty without a snapshot), featFreeCastUsedKey (slugifies the spell name; must match the backend key), featGrantRedundant (armor/weapon redundancy locks, not-redundant → null, ignores non-prof effects), featAbilityChoiceOptions (Resilient filters held saves, non-save half-feat unchanged) + abilityChoiceGrantsSave, getManeuverGrantSpec/maneuverGrantComplete (Martial Adept), getFeatManeuvers (from choices.maneuvers), martialAdeptDieCount/martialAdeptManeuverCount, maneuver_grant counts as mechanized, getFeatResources still surfaces the Martial Adept d6, getFeatProficiencyGrants surfaces Tavern Brawler's improvised-weapon proficiency (29 tests)
│   │   ├── featProficiencyData.js # Feat count-choice proficiency grants (Skilled 3 skills/tools, Linguist 3 languages, Weapon Master 4 weapons) + **Expertise** (Skill Expert) — mirrors subclassProficiencyData but feat-specific. Pools (FEAT_SKILL/TOOL/LANGUAGE/WEAPON_OPTIONS); getFeatProficiencyChoices(feat, {proficientSkills}) (count-choice + `expertise` effects, not fixed-item; the Expertise pool is the passed proficient skills — incl. a skill picked from the same feat), availableFeatOptions (hides already-held), applyFeatProficiencyChoice(prof_type, chosen, cd) → patch (skill→skill_proficiencies, expertise→expertise_skills, tool→feat_tool_proficiencies, language→feat_languages, weapon→feat_weapon_proficiencies, skill_or_tool split by membership); groupFeatProfOptions(prof_type, options) → [{category|null, options}] organizes a skill/tool/skill_or_tool grant's picker into labeled buckets (Skills · Artisan's Tools · Gaming Sets · Musical Instruments · Tools & Kits, in display order, empty ones dropped) so the Skilled feat reads by category instead of one flat list; other grant types (language/weapon/expertise) stay one uncategorized group; getFeatGrantedSkills(feats) → the skills recorded on each feat instance's `choices.skills` (deduped) so the Stats skills panel can flag feat-granted skills (a feat's chosen skills are snapshotted to `choices.skills` at acquisition by the LevelUpWizard feat step + Variant Human creation, since they otherwise merge indistinguishably into skill_proficiencies). Drives the count-limited pickers in the LevelUpWizard feat step + Variant Human creation — both pass `proficientSkills` so Skill Expert's Expertise pool populates (creation uses the Identity-step proficient set: background + race + Half-Elf/Variant + the skill this feat grants, since class skills aren't picked until the Features step). A grant whose available pool is genuinely empty auto-completes via min(count, available) and is hidden ✅

│   │   ├── LevelUpWizard.jsx    # Dynamic-step modal: HP → (Subclass, if unlocking at this level) → Features → (ASI/Feat steps, ASI levels) → (New Spells, known casters only) → Confirm; subclass step only appears when leveling exactly to the class's unlock level and no subclass chosen yet; the **Features step resolves subclass features** — the class feature tables carry generic placeholders at subclass-feature levels ("Martial Archetype Feature" → "You gain a feature from your Martial Archetype."), so when the chosen subclass grants a feature at the new level the wizard substitutes the REAL feature (name + description, with an amber subclass badge) from `SUBCLASS_DATA[class][edition][subclass]` (e.g. Champion L15 shows Superior Critical); falls back to the placeholder text when the subclass can't be resolved; New Spells step appears for KNOWN casters (Bard/Sorcerer/Warlock via KNOWN_CASTERS set, **or a known-caster SUBCLASS — Eldritch Knight via getSubclassCaster over `effectiveSubclass`, so choosing EK at the L3 subclass step adds the New Spells step in the same wizard run; targets come from the subclass caster's own cantripsKnownAt/spellsKnownAt instead of CLASS_PROGRESSION**) — shows target cantrips/spells-known counts at the new level (read from CLASS_PROGRESSION columns) + SpellList free-add/remove pickers for cantrips and known_spells (the SpellList `onRemove` makes spell/cantrip **swap-on-level-up** work — remove one, add another; the intro notes this) + **browse pickers** (`ClassSpellBrowser mode="learn"`, testids `cantrip-browser`/`spell-browser`) under each list so the player picks from the actual spell catalog: the list class is the character's own class for Bard/Sorcerer/Warlock or the subclass caster's `spellList` (Eldritch Knight → Wizard), capped at `maxKnownSpellLevel` (EK → maxCastableLevel of the third-caster slots at the new level; full casters → highest s1–s9 progression column with slots; Warlock → the `slot_level` Pact Magic column); race-granted cantrips (`computeRaceGrantedCantrips`) pass to the cantrip browser as `grantedSpells` so a class pick can't duplicate them; the free-text SpellList stays as the homebrew fallback; saves level, hp_max, subclass, and (known casters) cantrips/known_spells into character_data. **Eldritch Knight school rules** (see subclassCasterData.js): for a **5e** EK the spell step **splits into three PAGES** — one choice each, since stacking all three plus their browsers on one page is a wall of UI: `spells-cantrips` → `spells-restricted` → `spells-any`, labelled "Cantrips" / "Abjuration & Evocation" / "Any School" in the step indicator. **The cantrip page only appears when there is a cantrip decision to make**: a 5e EK's cantrips are permanent (no swapping), so it shows only on the levels that grant one (L3, L10) or when the character is owed one it never picked — leveling 3→4 goes straight to the leveled pages. (Where cantrips CAN be swapped — 2024 — the choice exists every level, so its combined page always shows them.) Each page carries only its own budget copy (`spell-step-intro`) and its own category block (`ek-section-{key}`, count `ek-count-{key}`, browser `ek-browser-{key}`); the restricted page's browser is school-filtered, the any-school page's is not (those are the slots earned at levels 3/8/14/20). Every other known caster (incl. the unrestricted 2024 EK) keeps the SINGLE combined `spells` page — both layouts compose the same blocks. Each pick RECORDS its slot into `character_data.ek_spell_slots`, so a swap is confined to its own category by the per-category counts alone (no separate swap mode). The **2024** EK has no restriction and keeps the single list (and writes no slot map). **Swapping:** both editions may drop one already-known leveled spell per level-up (`leveledSwapPerLevel`) — further removals are refused; 2024 may also drop one known cantrip, while **5e cantrips are permanent** (`ek-cantrip-permanent-note`; removal refused). The one-swap budget is shared **across** the two leveled pages (one spell per level, not one per page). **A spell you may not give up right now shows no remove affordance at all** — the lists/browsers take a `lockedSpells` prop (SpellList hides its X; ClassSpellBrowser shows a "Locked" badge instead of Remove), so a refusal is visible rather than a button that silently does nothing. Locked = the spells you already knew once this level's swap is spent (an `ek-swap-spent-note` explains it), plus a 5e EK's permanent cantrips. Spells picked DURING this level-up are never locked — you can always change the pick you just made. **Each page must be filled to its exact quota before Next unlocks** — an under-filled page (a spell you are entitled to but have not picked, or a swap started and not finished) blocks you, as does an over-filled one; an amber `spell-page-incomplete` note says how many are still missing ("Choose 2 more cantrips to continue."), so you cannot reach the Abjuration/Evocation page without having chosen your cantrips. **ASI/Feat steps** (driven by `campaign.asi_feat_mode`): at an ASI level, `asi_only` → just the ASI step; `asi_or_feat` (default) → an "ASI or Feat" choice step (`asi-choice-asi`/`asi-choice-feat`) then the chosen branch; `asi_and_feat` → both. The Feat step reuses `FeatPicker` over `featService.getFeats(campaign.id, edition)`; already-taken non-repeatable feats are excluded, and feats whose prerequisites aren't met (`checkFeatPrerequisite`, ability+level+spellcasting+armor buckets — spellcaster derived from class/caster-subclass/known-spells; armor proficiencies derived from class table + race grants + feat-granted armor) are shown **locked at the bottom of the list** (disabled, with the unmet reason) via FeatPicker's `getDisabledReason` prop rather than hidden; the chosen feat is appended to `character_data.feats` as `{id, name, level, effects?, choices?}` — its structured `effects` are snapshotted onto the instance (so the sheet's resolvers work without re-fetching) and a half-feat ability choice (e.g. Tavern Brawler +1 STR/CON, via `feat-ability-{stat}`) is recorded in `choices` and folded into the level's ability-score updates ✅

confirm-hp-breakdown itemizes "+2 CON + 3 retroactive CON"; no retro top-up when CON is unchanged); subclass-grants step (Battle Master adds it at L3 + saves subclass_tool_proficiencies, Next blocked until chosen, no step for Champion at L3, hides already-held tools); level-choices step (Sorcerer Metamagic: no step at a non-learn level, prompts the per-level delta at L3 + Next-blocked-until-chosen + appends to character_data.metamagic on confirm, hides already-known options at L9→10 + appends to the existing list; Warlock Eldritch Invocations: prompts the L2 delta, level-gates the pool — a L5 invocation isn't offered at L2 — and appends to character_data.eldritch_invocations on confirm; swap-on-level-up — replacing one known option raises the required count by 1 and removes it on confirm); subclass-grants step (Champion Additional Fighting Style: adds the step at L10 5e / L7 2024 + saves additional_fighting_styles, Next blocked until chosen, excludes the base fighting_style, no step at a non-grant level); redundant half-feats locked (Fighter → Weapon Master "all weapon proficiencies" + Moderately Armored "already proficient with medium armor"); Resilient ability chooser excludes saves the class already has (Fighter → no STR/CON option); maneuver-grant feat (Martial Adept) blocks Next until 2 picked + saves choices.maneuvers, non-Battle-Master doesn't merge into character_data.maneuvers, Battle Master merges picks + excludes known; Features step resolves subclass features (5e Champion L15 shows Superior Critical not the generic "Martial Archetype Feature" placeholder, 2024 too, keeps the placeholder when the subclass is unknown); Eldritch Knight New Spells (choosing EK at L3 adds the step in the same run + switching to Champion removes it, EK targets 2 cantrips/3 spells from the subclass progression, existing EK gets the step at a later level-up, onComplete carries the picked cantrips/known_spells (2024), no step for a Champion; browse pickers — EK L3 gets Wizard-list cantrip+spell browsers capped at spell level 1, browser picks flow into onComplete, Sorcerer gets a Sorcerer-list browser capped at its full-caster max, a High Elf's race-granted Fire Bolt passes to the cantrip browser as grantedSpells but not to the leveled-spell browser); Eldritch Knight spell schools (5e splits the leveled picker into Abjuration & Evocation + Any School sections with the right per-level counts, the restricted browser gets the school filter and the any-school one doesn't, a pick RECORDS its slot into ek_spell_slots, a spell shows in the slot it was recorded in even when its school says otherwise (Shield in the any slot), removing a restricted spell reopens a RESTRICTED slot only, only ONE leveled swap per level, Next blocked on a half-finished swap until refilled from the same category, 5e cantrips permanent / 2024 swaps one cantrip per level, 2024 has no split and stores no slot map, a known-caster CLASS (Sorcerer) is unaffected; page split — each choice gets its own page showing only its own budget/category, the one-swap budget is shared across the two leveled pages rather than one per page, a 2024 EK keeps one combined page; Next is blocked until the page quota is met + the "choose N more" note, and a half-finished swap on a full page blocks it too; the cantrip page is hidden on a 5e level that grants no cantrip, shown at L10 when one IS gained (old cantrips still permanent) and when the character is owed one; a spent swap LOCKS the spells you already knew (no remove button + a note) while a spell picked this level-up stays changeable, and the any-school spell is swappable at L4 past the ASI step) (102 tests)

Fetches the catalog (encyclopediaService, CampaignContext edition) to learn class spell levels; **Racial** (`spell-source-racial-content`, race cantrips) + **Feats** (`spell-source-feats-content`, feat spells at that level + the `featTrackers` node) are rendered here, while **class content is delegated** via `renderClass(level|null, {atLevel, levelMap})` so each caster keeps its own list shape (EK Abj/Evo split, prepared-caster slot-cast list). Flat stacked fallback (class → racial → feats) until the catalog loads or when spells span ≤1 level. Props campaignId/classCantrips/classLeveledNames/racialCantrips/featCantrips/featLeveled/featTrackers/characterLevel/spellSaveDc/spellAttackBonus/renderClass. Consumed by BOTH CasterSpellBlock blocks (known = EK, prepared = Wizard). ✅

│   │   ├── SpellAddPicker.jsx  # The ONLY way to add a spell to a character. A collapsed "+ Add a spell/cantrip" button that expands a learn-mode `ClassSpellBrowser` scoped to the right class list + spell levels (`minSpellLevel`/`maxSpellLevel` — 0/0 = cantrips only; optional `schools` restriction for the 5e Eldritch Knight; `limit` = max known, null = uncapped). Replaces the old free-text add input that used to live in `SpellList` — **a character may only know spells that exist in the compendium**, so there is no "type a spell name" input anywhere in the app any more. Homebrew reaches a character the same way: the GM authors the spell into the campaign compendium and it appears in this picker like any other. Consumed by every caster surface that adds spells (the 15 hand-written caster sheets, CasterSpellBlock, FeatSpellsSection's ritual book). Props className/campaignId/spells/limit/onAdd/onRemove/minSpellLevel/maxSpellLevel/schools/label/testId ✅

│   │   ├── spellUpcast.js      # Pure best-effort upcast summary for the Cast dialog (no React/imports, so the Node report reuses it). `spellSaveAbility(desc)`/`spellUsesAttackRoll(desc)` (detect save ability / attack roll from description prose), `computeUpcastDice(spell, chosenLevel)` (parse the "Nd_ per slot level above X" increment from `higher_level` + the SAME-die base dice from `description` → exact dice at the chosen level; returns null for count/duration/DC upcasts, `{ambiguous:true}` when multiple differing base dice share the die size — never guesses), `summarizeUpcast(spell, chosenLevel, {saveDc, attackBonus})` → `{isUpcast, hasHigherLevel, higherLevelText, noExtraEffect, save:{ability,dc}|null, attackBonus, damage:{base,atLevel,kind}|null}`, `classifyUpcast(spell)` → `{status:'none'|'computed'|'prose-only', reason?}` (drives the coverage report). **Cantrip scaling** (cantrips scale with CHARACTER level, not slots): `cantripDamageAtLevel(spell, characterLevel)` reads the standard "damage increases by 1d10 when you reach 5th level (2d10), 11th level (3d10), and 17th level (4d10)" sentence LITERALLY (base die + each parenthetical tier, highest tier ≤ level) → `{base, atLevel, die}`; null for beam-scaling (Eldritch Blast) / non-damage cantrips. `summarizeCantrip(spell, characterLevel, {saveDc, attackBonus})` → `{damage, save, attackBonus}` for the cantrip row. Consumed by SpellList's Cast dialog + cantrip rows + `report-upcast-coverage.mjs` ✅

```

### Auth + Campaign Context Pattern
- `AuthContext` — wraps the entire app; calls `/api/auth/me` on mount; `user` is null until resolved; exposes `setUser` so `Login` can update context without a page reload
- `CampaignContext` — `campaign` shape: `{id, name, description, edition, created_by, userRole: 'gm'|'player', ...}`
  - `userRole` is computed in CampaignSelection: `campaign.created_by === user.id ? 'gm' : 'player'`
  - `created_by` comes from `CampaignListItem` (the list endpoint includes it — required for role computation)
  - Persisted to localStorage so page refreshes restore context automatically
  - `authService.logout()` clears `selectedCampaign` from localStorage to prevent stale role surviving across sessions
- `MainLayout` — heals stale `userRole` on every mount: re-derives role from `campaign.created_by === user.id` and calls `enterCampaign` if the stored value is wrong. Guards against sessions where role was incorrectly persisted.
- `isGm` in pages/components: always use `campaign?.userRole === 'gm'`, never `user.is_admin`
- `ProtectedRoute` — redirects unauthenticated users to `/login` while `loading` is true shows nothing

| `/campaigns/:campaignId/encyclopedia/mechanics/armor-class` | ArmorClassPage | ✅ Functional (static AC reference: armor categories/shields/unarmored defenses/feat AC mods/armor Strength requirements (−10 ft speed when unmet)/armor proficiency (STR-DEX disadvantage + no casting when worn unproficient), worked examples via computeArmorClass; linked from the Items-tab InventoryTab AC summary "Learn more") |
| `/campaigns/:campaignId/encyclopedia/mechanics/action-economy` | ActionEconomyPage | ✅ Functional (static action-economy reference: the 5 buckets via TABS/TAB_LABELS, standard actions bound to UNIVERSAL_ACTIONS_*, edition toggle since the action menu differs; linked from the Action Economy tab "Learn more") |
| `/campaigns/:campaignId/encyclopedia/mechanics/hit-dice` | HitDicePage | ✅ Functional (static hit-dice reference: pool = level × class die, short-rest spend = die+CON, long-rest recovery = half total rounded down min 1, Durable floor via durableHitDieMin, Bard Song of Rest; linked from the Stats-tab Hit Points & Movement card "How Hit Dice work") |
| `/campaigns/:campaignId/encyclopedia/mechanics/loading` | LoadingPage | ✅ Functional (static Loading-property reference, edition toggle since 2024 removed the property: one shot per action/bonus/reaction regardless of Extra Attack, the 4 loading weapons, Crossbow Expert removes it on proficient crossbows + Action Surge/War Priest as extra-action workarounds; note text sourced from `weaponLoadingNote`; linked from the Items tab + Action Economy tab loading notes) |
| `/campaigns/:campaignId/encyclopedia/mechanics/object-interaction` | ObjectInteractionPage | ✅ Functional (static "Drawing & Stowing Weapons" reference — pure rules, no helper/card, no edition toggle: one free object interaction per turn, swapping weapons = two interactions, Dual Wielder draws/stows two; cross-links Loading/AC/Action Economy; linked from the Dual Wielder feat (FeatsTab dialog + FeatsSubTab row) and the Items-tab Weapons sub-tab) |
| `/campaigns/:campaignId/encyclopedia/mechanics/spacing` | SpacingPage | ✅ Functional (static "Spacing & the 5-Foot Rule" reference — pure rules, no helper/card, no edition toggle: what "within 5 ft" means, opportunity attacks + Disengage, disadvantage on ranged weapon AND ranged spell attacks within 5 ft, and the in-app feats/features that bend these rules (Crossbow Expert etc.); cross-links Action Economy + Loading; linked from each ranged weapon row in the Items tab, the CharacterDetail Spells tab, and the Action Economy tab (per ranged weapon entry + the Spell section)) |

### Locations UI — Key Behaviours
- **Maps tab:** thumbnail strip (left) + scrollable map viewer (right) with zoom (+/−/scroll wheel) and dark background
- **Pins:** click to open tooltip (toggle), X to close; linked pins are blue and navigate to linked location
- **Pin dialog:** link to existing location OR create a new one inline
- **Player view toggle:** filters to `is_visible_to_players=true` locations/maps/pins/NPCs; auto-selects first visible map
- **Map upload:** 100 MB limit; client-side size validation with inline error before upload attempt
- Maps default to `is_visible_to_players=false` — GM must explicitly show each map to players
- **Info tab — GM view:** always editable; section cards for Details, GM Notes (private), Lore & Culture, Environment, Adventure, Hierarchy, Important NPCs; Save/Reset buttons per card
- **Info tab — Player view:** read-only; empty sections hidden entirely; GM Notes never shown; NPC cards only show NPCs where `is_visible_to_players=true` on the NPC
- **Important NPCs:** two sources merged in the NPC list response — `source="linked"` (manual `location_npcs` junction, has role description) and `source="last_seen"` (NPC's `last_known_location_id` points here, shows `last_seen_notes`). Deduped: if an NPC is both, `linked` wins. NPC cards show portrait thumbnail (left strip, 64px wide, if `image_path` exists), name, race, occupation, role/last_seen description, and NPC `summary`. Cards are clickable and navigate to `/campaigns/:campaignId/npcs/:npcId`. GM remove button uses `stopPropagation` so it doesn't trigger navigation.
- **Timeline Events card (Info tab):** loaded on page mount via `GET /timeline?location_id=N`; shown in both GM and player views; GM view always shows the card (empty state: "No timeline events linked") with visibility badges for hidden events; player view shows only when events exist (visible-only events); each row shows title + era dates (`year abbreviation` separated by `·`) or "Unknown date" italic; events are managed from the Campaign Time page
- **Sessions card (Info tab):** loaded on mount via `GET /sessions?location_id=N`; GM view always shows (empty state: "No session notes linked to this location.") with Hidden badges; player view shows only when sessions exist; each row shows session title + real_world_date + Hidden badge (GM); clickable → SessionDetail
- **Hierarchy card (GM only):** set top-level, set parent, mark as unknown; child list shows manually-set children (link icon) and pin-derived children (pin icon + "via map pin" label)
- **LocationList — GM view:** three sections — World Hierarchy tree (from top-level), Unsorted (staging area, locations not placed anywhere), Unknown Locations. When the top-level location has maps, a GM toolbar row appears above the map with an "Edit [name]" button that navigates to that location's `LocationDetail` page for full editing (info, maps, pins).
- **LocationList — Player view:** toggle Hierarchy / A–Z; search bar; Unknown Locations section at bottom of hierarchy view
- **Hierarchy tree:** indented expandable tree; click node to navigate; pin-derived children included automatically from `map_pins.linked_location_id`
- **Unsorted locations:** visible to GM only; any location with no parent, not top-level, not unknown, not pin-linked from any other location's map
- **`pin_child_ids`:** computed at query time in `get_locations` — for each location, the IDs of locations linked via pins on that location's maps
- **Pin → parent persistence:** when a GM creates or updates a pin with a `linked_location_id`, the service automatically sets the linked location's `parent_location_id` to the map's parent location — but only if the linked location has no parent yet, is not top-level, and is not unknown ("first pin wins"). This makes the hierarchy fully explicit in the DB.
- **Top-level map on LocationList:** if a top-level location has maps, the first map is displayed above the hierarchy tree as a read-only image preview (no pins); multiple maps show a tab strip; a GM toolbar above the map shows an "Edit [name]" button to navigate to LocationDetail for full editing; respects player view filter (only visible maps shown to players)

### NPCs UI — Key Behaviours
- **NPCList grid:** portrait thumbnail (placeholder icon when none), color-coded status badge (alive/dead/missing/unknown), race · occupation subtitle, summary text
- **GM controls on card:** eye toggle (visibility) + delete button overlaid on portrait; Player View preview toggle
- **Filters:** search bar (matches name, race, occupation, and summary text) + location dropdown + status dropdown + sort select
- **NPCList purpose:** acts as a searchable NPC database — designed for cross-cutting queries ("what blacksmiths have we found?", "who was that bandit we killed?") rather than geographic browsing (use Locations for that)
- **Location filter:** flat dropdown of all campaign locations; selecting a location shows NPCs whose `last_known_location_id` is anywhere in that location's subtree (walks `parent_location_id` + `pin_child_ids` recursively on the client); "Unplaced" option shows NPCs with no `last_known_location_id`
- **Sort options:** Name A–Z (default) | Name Z–A | Recently added (by id desc) | Status (alive → missing → unknown → dead)
- **Create dialog:** name (required), race, occupation, alignment, status select, summary, visible-to-players checkbox; navigates directly to NPCDetail on create
- **NPCDetail portrait:** click or drag-drop to upload (10 MB limit, client-side check); remove button; upload overlay while in progress
- **NPCDetail core fields:** name, status, race, occupation, alignment, visibility checkbox — inline editing in portrait row; per-section Save/Reset appear only when dirty
- **Info tab sections:** Physical (age, gender, height, weight, appearance) | Personality (voice, traits, ideals, bonds, flaws, languages as removable tags) | Narrative (summary, description, backstory — labeled "Player visible") | GM Notes (amber-tinted "Private" card, GM only) | Location & Media (last known location select, last-seen notes, theme music — paste-a-link input + "Upload audio" button via `musicInputRef`/`npcService.uploadMusic` + Remove + inline `<MusicPlayer>` that plays Spotify/YouTube/uploaded audio in-browser; the old quick-info grid "Listen" link was removed in favour of the player) | Combat Stats (freeform JSON textarea, GM only) | Timeline Events (loaded on mount via `GET /timeline?npc_id=N`)
- **Timeline Events card (Info tab — NPCDetail):** GM view always shows the card (with count) and a `+` toggle button in the header; clicking `+` shows an inline form with two modes — "Link existing" (select from all campaign events not already linked, optional description) and "Create new" (title required, description, era/year/month/day date fields, visible-to-players checkbox); each event row has a `×` unlink button (GM only); remove fetches the junction link ID via `getEventNpcs` then calls `removeEventNpc`; player view shows only when events exist and has no add/remove controls; event titles link to the Timeline page; data-testid `timeline-events-toggle` on `+` button, `unlink-event-{id}` on remove buttons
- **Sessions card (Info tab — NPCDetail):** loaded on mount via `GET /sessions?npc_id=N`; GM view always shows (empty state: "No session notes linked to this NPC.") with Hidden badges; player view shows only when sessions exist; each row shows `#N — Title` (or just title if no number) + real_world_date; clickable → SessionDetail
- **Player view:** all GM-only cards (GM Notes, Combat Stats, Player Relationships tab) hidden; all fields read-only; empty sections suppressed entirely; Timeline Events and Sessions cards hidden when no events/sessions
- **Relationships tab:** NPC-to-NPC list; related NPC name is a clickable link to their detail page; GM can add (select NPC + type + description) and delete
- **Player Relationships tab (GM only):** NPC-to-player list; populated from campaign members (players only); GM can add and delete
- **Language tags:** add via input + Enter or button, remove with × on each tag; stored as JSONB array
- **Last Known Location:** select from all campaign locations; in player view shows as a clickable link to that location's detail page
- **Portrait display note:** images are served via `app.mount("/uploads", StaticFiles(...))` in `main.py` — upload/delete/display are all functional

### Campaign Settings UI — Key Behaviours
- **Route:** `/campaigns/:campaignId/settings` — wrapped in `<MainLayout>`; GM-only editing controls; accessible to all members (read-only for players); "Campaign Settings" link (Settings icon) in GM section of Sidebar
- **General tab:** Campaign Identity section (name, description, edition select — 5e/5.5e), Roleplaying Options (alignment toggle — hides alignment from all character forms when off), Ability Score Assignment (method select: Standard Array / Point Buy / Dice Roll; conditional "Allow reroll 1s" toggle appears when Dice Roll chosen), Leveling section (Milestone / Experience XP select + Ability Score Improvements select — `data-testid="asi-feat-mode-select"`: "Ability score increase only" = `asi_only` | "...or a feat" = `asi_or_feat` | "...and a feat" = `asi_and_feat`; drives the LevelUpWizard ASI-level steps), Currency section (coin-types select — "Copper, Silver, Gold, Platinum" = `standard` vs "All five (adds Electrum)" = `full`; `data-testid="currency-type-select"`), Starting Equipment section (`data-testid="starting-equipment-select"` — "Class & background equipment" = `equipment` | "Equipment, or take starting gold" = `equipment_or_gold` | "Nothing (no equipment or gold)" = `none`; drives the CharacterCreate Equipment step); Save/Reset buttons appear per section when dirty; players see read-only view of settings (currency shows "CP, SP, GP, PP" / "CP, SP, EP, GP, PP"; starting equipment shows "Class & background equipment" / "Equipment or starting gold" / "None"; ASI mode shows "Ability score increase or a feat" etc. via `asi-feat-mode-readonly`)
- **Members tab:** full member management (extracted from old /members page); GM section (crown icon, "You" badge); players list with invite/remove; invite search (≥2 chars, debounced); same `data-testid` attributes as CampaignMembers.jsx

### Calendar/Timeline Settings UI — Key Behaviours
- **Route:** `/campaigns/:campaignId/campaign-time` — wrapped in `<MainLayout>` in App.jsx (not internally); GM-only controls; "Campaign Time" link in GM section of Sidebar
- **Calendar tab:** landing page (explainer + defaults bullet list + "Set Up Calendar" button) when 404; management UI when calendar exists
  - **General card:** calendar name, days_per_month, `use_weeks` toggle (ToggleLeft/ToggleRight icon), `days_per_week` input (shown only when `use_weeks=true`); Save button appears when dirty
  - **Seasons card:** inline editable list (click name to rename), add via input+Enter/button, delete with confirmation; deletion nullifies month assignments in local state
  - **Months card:** one row per month — ordinal (read-only), optional name input (placeholder "Month N" when blank), season select; Save button appears per row when dirty; `monthLabel()` helper returns name or "Month N"
  - **Weekdays card:** only shown when `use_weeks=true`; same add/edit/delete pattern as seasons; shows order_index as prefix
